Title: Della regolata divozione de' cristiani, trattato di Lamindo Pritanio
Creator: Lodovico Antonio Muratori, writing as Lamindo Pritanio
Publisher: In Trento: per Gianbattista Parone, stampatore vescovile
Publication Date: 1748
Rights: Public Domain (published 1748, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/')
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_XGb89Fg3TwMC (https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_XGb89Fg3TwMC), the item's own bub_gb_XGb89Fg3TwMC_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: National Library of Naples; host metadata date: 1748. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 551f7a2693c7c413230998e8f8999d0b859d4990701e7f4d167979cb0df267be.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Google Books scan from the National Library of Naples, re-hosted on the Internet Archive; good OCR with the long s read as f and 'Digitized by Google' lines left in place.
Content note: Muratori's treatise on well-ordered devotion, published under his pen name in 1747: a priest-scholar's call to centre piety on God, the liturgy and charity rather than excess cults; the founding text of the Catholic Enlightenment in devotion.
